### [95] [The Minimal High-Quality QCD Axion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08814)
*Mario Fernández Navarro*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种最小的高质量QCD轴子模型，其中Peccei-Quinn对称性起源于五维U(1)规范对称性在平坦区间上的边界剩余，轴子作为威尔逊线相位存在，无需体QCD、翘曲或色Chern-Simons项，同时保持指数级质量保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的QCD轴子虽然简单但质量保护不足，本文旨在寻找一个既最小化又具有高质量保护的QCD轴子模型。

Method: 通过将Peccei-Quinn对称性视为五维U(1)规范对称性在平坦区间上的边界剩余，轴子作为威尔逊线相位出现，QCD和标准模型保持四维，保留了KSVZ/DFSZ反常扇区的简洁性。

Result: 该构造无需体QCD、翘曲或色Chern-Simons项，就能实现指数级质量保护，证明轴子质量保护与最小性可以兼容。

Conclusion: 本文表明，通过引入额外维度的规范对称性，可以在保持模型最小化的同时实现高质量的QCD轴子，解决了标准轴子模型质量保护不足的问题。

Abstract: The canonical QCD axion is minimal but not high quality. We identify a minimal high-quality QCD axion, in which the Peccei-Quinn symmetry emerges as a gauge-origin boundary remnant of five-dimensional $U(1)$ gauge invariance on a flat interval, and the axion is the surviving Wilson-line phase. QCD and the Standard Model remain four-dimensional, retaining much of the simplicity of KSVZ/DFSZ anomaly sectors. The construction requires no bulk QCD, warping, or color Chern-Simons term, yet retains exponential quality protection. Thus axion quality is compatible with minimality.

</details>


### [96] [High Energy Photon Polarimetry at Lepton Colliders: Quantum Information from Converted Photons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08816)
*Carlos Henrique de Lima,Navin McGinnis,David McKeen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在轻子对撞机中产生的高能光子在探测器内转换为正负电子对的过程，作为测量量子信息可观测量的一种工具。研究涉及e+e-→γγ和e+e-→e+e-γ过程中的单转换和双转换过程。单转换可在原位提取自旋分析能力，双转换则探测极化关联。以Belle-II探测器为例，研究表明根据转换正负电子对张角的重建分辨率，可以探测双光子系统的量子关联，特别是可以在高显著性下测量贝尔不等式违背、量子失协、共生性、非稳定性和导引性等量子关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用轻子对撞机中高能光子转换为正负电子对的过程，开发一种新的实验工具来测量量子信息可观测量，实现对GeV尺度光子的量子关联特性的实验探测。

Method: 研究e+e-→γγ和e+e-→e+e-γ过程中的单转换和双转换过程，以Belle-II探测器为应用场景，分析转换正负电子对张角重建分辨率对量子关联探测能力的影响。

Result: 单转换过程可原位提取自旋分析能力，双转换过程可探测极化关联；在Belle-II探测器上，通过合适的分辨率可高显著性探测贝尔不等式违背、量子失协、共生性、非稳定性和导引性等量子关联。

Conclusion: 利用光子到正负电子对的转换过程，可以在GeV尺度上实现对空间分离光子的量子关联的高显著性测量，为高能物理中的量子信息研究提供了新的实验途径。

Abstract: We study high-energy photons produced at a lepton collider that convert into an $e^+e^-$ pair in the detector, as a tool for measuring quantum information observables. We consider single- and double-conversion processes in $e^+e^- \to γγ$ and $e^+e^- \to e^+e^-γ$. Single conversions enable an in situ extraction of the spin-analyzing power, while double conversions probe polarization correlations. Focusing on the Belle-II detector, we show that, depending on the reconstruction resolution of the opening angle of the conversion $e^+e^-$ pair, quantum correlations of the diphoton system can be probed. In particular, measurements of violations of the Bell inequality, quantum discord, concurrence, nonstabilizerness, and steerability with spatially separated GeV-scale photons can be made at high significance.

</details>


### [97] [Renormalon subtracted nonrelativistic QCD for heavy hadron systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08817)
*Benoît Assi,Andreas S. Kronfeld,Simon Vaiva,Michael L. Wagman*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种重正子减除形式的pNRQCD方法，结合VMC/GFMC方法与NNLO静态两体和三体势，用于重强子体系的精密光谱学。通过最小重正子减除(MRS)系统求和阶乘增长项，稳定了微扰收敛性并降低了重整化标度依赖性。在调谐粲夸克和底夸克质量后，预测了全重重子($\Omega_{ccc}$, $\Omega_{ccb}$, $\Omega_{cbb}$, $\Omega_{bbb}$)以及含顶夸克的QCD稳定重子的质量。NNLO MRS结果比晶格QCD低125-175 MeV，分数差异随$1/m_Q$递减。将该方法应用于不等质量全重四夸克，确定了束缚所需的重轻质量比临界值，并计算了质量比全景观的束缚能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有pNRQCD方法在全重重子/四夸克等强子系统的精密质量预测中存在微扰收敛性差和重整化标度依赖性强的问题，需要发展更系统的重正子减除方案来提高预测精度和可靠性。

Method: 采用重正子减除形式的pNRQCD，结合变分蒙特卡罗(VMC)和格林函数蒙特卡罗(GFMC)方法，使用NNLO阶静态两体和三体势。应用最小重正子减除(MRS)系统求和阶乘增长项，通过调谐粲和底夸克质量到自旋平均的1S夸克偶素态进行参数固定。

Result: 预测了$\Omega_{ccc}$, $\Omega_{ccb}$, $\Omega_{cbb}$, $\Omega_{bbb}$全重重子质量和含顶夸克QCD稳定重子质量。NNLO MRS结果比晶格QCD低125-175 MeV，分数差异随$1/m_Q$递减。确定了不等质量全重四夸克束缚所需的重轻质量比临界值，并给出了质量比全景观的束缚能。

Conclusion: MRS方案有效改善了pNRQCD微扰收敛性，预测值与晶格QCD的差异主要来自被忽略的$\mathcal{O}(1/m_Q)$修正。该方法可推广到不等质量全重四夸克体系，为全重强子谱提供系统理论框架。

Abstract: We present a renormalon-subtracted formulation of potential nonrelativistic QCD (pNRQCD) for precision spectroscopy of heavy hadron systems, combining variational and Green's function Monte Carlo (VMC/GFMC) methods with NNLO static two- and three-body potentials. Minimal renormalon subtraction (MRS) systematically sums leading factorially growing terms, thereby stabilizing perturbative convergence and reducing renormalization-scale dependence. We tune charm and bottom quark masses to spin-averaged $1S$ quarkonium states and predict $Ω_{ccc}$, $Ω_{ccb}$, $Ω_{cbb}$, and $Ω_{bbb}$ baryon masses, as well as QCD-stable baryons containing top quarks. NNLO MRS results undershoot lattice QCD by 125--175~MeV, with fractional differences decreasing as $\sim 1/m_Q$, consistent with neglected $\mathcal{O}(1/m_Q)$ corrections. Applying these methods to unequal-mass fully-heavy tetraquarks, we determine the critical heavy-to-light mass ratio for binding and compute binding energies across the mass-ratio landscape.

</details>


### [98] [Seesaw reheating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08828)
*Yann Mambrini*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"跷跷板再加热"场景，其中暴胀子将能量传递给与轻子数自发破缺相关的长寿命中间标量，再通过其衰变为右手中微子完成宇宙再加热，使再加热温度由跷跷板扇区动力学而非暴胀子衰变宽度决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准再加热场景中再加热温度由暴胀子衰变宽度直接决定，本文旨在引入一个中间标量场，使得再加热温度由跷跷板机制的动力学决定，从而建立宇宙再加热与中微子质量起源及暗物质之间的自然联系。

Method: 推导描述完整再加热历史的解析解，分析了中间标量的相对论性时间膨胀效应（抑制衰变、延迟能量传递）及其从相对论到非相对论区的转变（引入新特征时间尺度），最终得到再加热温度的简洁解析表达式。

Result: 揭示了中间标量的相对论时间膨胀效应和相对论-非相对论转变两个特征，得到了再加热温度的简洁解析表达式。

Conclusion: 当中间标量与轻子数自发破缺场对应时，该框架自然地将再加热温度与中微子质量起源联系起来，并为惰性中微子暗物质提供了良好动机的理论设定。

Abstract: We introduce the Seesaw Reheating scenario, in which the inflaton transfers its energy to a long-lived intermediate scalar associated with the spontaneous breaking of lepton number before the Universe is reheated through its decay into right-handed neutrinos. As a result, the reheating temperature is no longer determined by the inflaton decay width but by the dynamics of the seesaw sector. We derive analytical solutions describing the complete reheating history, revealing two characteristic features of this scenario: the relativistic time dilation of the intermediate scalar, which suppresses its decay and delays the transfer of energy to the thermal bath, and its subsequent transition from a relativistic to a non-relativistic regime, introducing a new characteristic timescale in the thermal history. Together, these effects lead to simple analytical expressions for the reheating temperature. When the intermediate scalar is identified with the field responsible for the spontaneous breaking of lepton number, the same framework naturally connects the reheating temperature to the origin of neutrino masses and provides a well-motivated setting for sterile-neutrino dark matter.

</details>


### [99] [Flavorful Interactions of AGN Neutrinos with Dark Matter Spike](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08833)
*S. Abbaslu,Y. Farzan,A. Maleki*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该研究针对IceCube-Gen2即将实现高能宇宙中微子精确味比测量这一前景，构建了中微子与暗物质相互作用的模型，并研究了活动星系核周围暗物质尖峰对中微子味比测量的影响。研究表明，IceCube-Gen2的味测量能够区分该模型与标准预期(1/3:1/3:1/3)以及阻尼μ子源的预测。通过结合味比测量、地面实验确定的中微子质量顺序以及τ衰变模式测量中偏离标准模型的潜在迹象，可以推导出暗物质尖峰及其组成粒子的特征信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IceCube-Gen2将实现高能宇宙中微子精确味比测量，这为探测中微子与暗物质的非标准相互作用提供了前所未有的机会。活动星系核周围的暗物质尖峰可能显著影响中微子传播过程中的味比，从而成为一种潜在的暗物质探测新途径。

Method: 构建中微子与暗物质相互作用的理论模型，研究活动星系核周围暗物质尖峰对宇宙中微子味比的影响，并分析IceCube-Gen2对该模型的区分能力。同时提出结合味比测量、中微子质量顺序和τ衰变模式等多方面信息来约束暗物质尖峰参数。

Result: IceCube-Gen2的味比测量能够区分该中微子-暗物质相互作用模型与标准预期(1/3:1/3:1/3)以及阻尼μ子源预测。通过联合分析味比测量、中微子质量顺序和τ衰变模式测量，可以提取暗物质尖峰及其组成粒子的特征信息。

Conclusion: IceCube-Gen2有望通过精确味比测量检验中微子-暗物质相互作用模型，并联合多种观测手段揭示活动星系核周围暗物质尖峰的性质，为暗物质研究开辟新的窗口。

Abstract: IceCube-Gen2 is going to make the dream of precise flavor ratio measurement for high energy cosmic neutrinos a reality. Motivated by this prospect, we build a model for the interaction of neutrinos with the dark matter and study the impact of the neutrino interaction with the dark matter spike around active galactic nuclei on the neutrino flavor ratio measurement. We show that the flavor measurement by IceCube-Gen2 can discriminate between this model and the standard expectation, $(ν_e^\oplus:ν_μ^\oplus:ν_τ^\oplus)\simeq (1/3:1/3:1/3)$, as well as the prediction for a damped muon source. We discuss how we can derive information about the spike as well as about the characteristics of the dark matter particles composing it by combining the flavor ratio measurements with the results of the terrestrial experiments determining the neutrino mass ordering and a potential deviation from the standard model predictions in the measurements of the tau decay modes.

</details>


### [100] [Dark Phoenix: dark matter relic from its own decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08838)
*Debasish Borah,Satyabrata Mahapatra,Indrajit Saha,Narendra Sahu,Vicky Singh Thounaojam*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 提出"暗黑凤凰"机制：DM在传统冻结超出后，通过有限温度效应导致的瞬态衰变窗口减少丰度，随后由一阶相变终止衰变，使暗物质 relic 符合观测值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统冻结-out机制中暗物质热产生过量的问题，提供一种无需精细调节即可使DM relic密度降至观测值的新途径。

Method: 利用有限温度效应使DM（ψ）在冻结后短暂重于暗扇区伙伴φ，开启衰变窗口降低丰度；随后由标量η驱动的一阶相变使φ质量突增，关闭衰变；期间φ通过足够强的对湮灭避免其后衰变重新过量产生DM。

Result: DM直接探测率因小耦合被抑制；带电标量φ可作为长寿命带电径迹在对撞机上被观测；FOPT产生的引力波信号可被LISA等未来实验探测。

Conclusion: Dark Phoenix机制为暗物质热产生过量问题提供了可行的解决方案，并具有直接探测抑制、对撞机长寿命带电粒子及引力波等多重可检验信号。

Abstract: We propose a novel mechanism for generating correct relic of dark matter (DM) which otherwise gets thermally overproduced from the conventional freeze-out mechanism. The mechanism, dubbed as {\it Dark Phoenix}, relies on a transient decay window for DM after its freeze-out which brings its relic within observed limits. Due to finite-temperature effects on masses, DM $ψ$ becomes heavier than its dark sector partner $φ$ in this window allowing it to decay. The decay of DM then stops after $φ$ gets a sudden jump in its mass from a first-order phase transition (FOPT) driven by another scalar $η$. The dark sector partner $φ$, assumed to be a charged scalar, undergoes sufficient pair annihilation during this epoch such that its late decay into DM does not overproduce the latter again. While direct-detection rates of DM remains suppressed due to small couplings, the charged scalar $φ$ can have interesting signatures like long-lived charged track at colliders. The associated FOPT can also lead to observable gravitational waves at future experiments like LISA.

</details>


### [101] [Dimensional Regularisation with Non-Anticommuting $γ_5$: Status and Application to the Standard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08847)
*Matthias Weißwange*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统阐述了Breitenlohner-Maison/'t Hooft-Veltman (BMHV)方案在维度正规化中对γ₅的一致全阶处理，重点报告了从D维实现、多圈行为（至4圈）到最新的完整1圈标准模型重整化（包括发散和有限对称性恢复抵消项）的研究现状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度电弱计算需要在维度正规化中对γ₅进行一致的全阶处理，但BMHV方案会破坏规范不变性和BRST不变性，必须通过对称性恢复抵消项来修复。

Method: 采用BMHV方案进行维度正规化，从D维实现出发，分析多圈行为至4圈阶，并应用于标准模型1圈重整化，计算发散和有限对称性恢复抵消项。

Result: 给出了BMHV方案在标准模型1圈重整化中的完整抵消项，包括发散和有限部分，并展示了代表性结果。

Conclusion: BMHV方案是实现高精度电弱计算中γ₅一致处理的关键，尽管会破坏对称性，但可通过对称性恢复抵消项加以修正，目前已完成至标准模型1圈重整化。

Abstract: A consistent all-order treatment of $γ_5$ in dimensional regularisation (DReg), as provided by the Breitenlohner-Maison/'t Hooft-Veltman (BMHV) scheme, is essential for high-precision electroweak calculations, but comes at the cost of a regularisation-induced violation of gauge and BRST invariance that must be reinstated via symmetry-restoring counterterms. We report on the current status of our research on the BMHV scheme, from its $D$-dimensional realisation and its multi-loop behaviour up to the 4-loop order, to its most recent application: the complete 1-loop renormalisation of the Standard Model (SM), including both divergent and finite symmetry-restoring counterterms. For the latter, we discuss the main intricacies of the BMHV treatment and present a representative excerpt of the resulting counterterms.

</details>


### [102] [Revisiting bounds on neutrino dark matter interaction at spikes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08904)
*Yasaman Farzan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文指出，高能中微子与活动星系核中心超大质量黑洞周围暗物质密集尖峰中的暗物质粒子相互作用导致的中微子通量衰减，强烈依赖于微观物理参数的细节。特别是当相互作用的媒介粒子是质量远小于中微子-暗物质质心能量的矢量玻色子时，中微子的能量损失及通量衰减可以忽略，从而放松了此前基于该过程导出的中微子-暗物质散射截面的约束边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的研究利用活动星系核产生的高能中微子与暗物质相互作用导致的通量衰减，推导了中微子-暗物质散射截面的约束。本文旨在证明这些约束强烈依赖于微观物理参数的细节，并指出在某些条件下约束会显著放松。

Method: 通过分析中微子与暗物质相互作用的微观物理机制，特别是考虑不同质量的媒介粒子（如矢量玻色子）对散射过程的影响，研究其能量损失特性及对通量衰减的依赖关系。

Result: 当媒介粒子为矢量玻色子且其质量远小于中微子-暗物质质心能量时，中微子的能量损失可以忽略，通量衰减极小，导致此前导出的散射截面约束边界被显著放松。

Conclusion: 中微子-暗物质散射截面的约束对微观物理参数（如媒介粒子质量）高度敏感，不能简单依赖于通量衰减观测，需要更细致的模型依赖分析。

Abstract: Interactions between high energy neutrinos produced in active galactic nuclei (AGNs) and the dark matter (DM) particles in the dense spike around the supermassive black holes may lead to attenuation of the flux. This consideration along with the observation of a sizeable flux from at least four AGNs have been used to derive bounds on the neutrino dark matter scattering cross section \cite{Cline:2023tkp,Cline:2022qld,Dixit:2026zkv}. We show that the attenuation strongly depends on the details of the microphysics parameters. In particular if the mediator is a vector boson with a mass much smaller than the center of mass energy of the neutrino and dark matter, the energy loss of the neutrino and therefore the flux attenuation will be negligible, relaxing the bounds.

</details>


### [103] [Production of hidden-charm molecular candidates in $ψ(4660)$ decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09093)
*Xiao-Yun Wang,Ya-Rong Wang,Shi-Dong Liu,Xiao-Hai Liu,Gang Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文假设多个隐藏粲奇特候选态（Z_c(3900)、Z_c(4020)、Z_cs(3985)、Z_2(4250)）主要为强子分子态，在ψ(4660)作为常规ψ(5S)粲偶素态的框架下，通过中间粲介子三角形圈机制研究其在ψ(4660)衰变中的产生，预测分支比分别为10^{-2}、10^{-4}、10^{-3}和10^{-6}量级，表明ψ(4660)衰变为探测带电隐藏粲奇特态的分子性质提供了有前景的平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探索隐藏粲奇特候选态（如Z_c(3900)、Z_c(4020)、Z_cs(3985)、Z_2(4250)）在ψ(4660)衰变中的产生机制，以验证这些态是否具有强子分子态的本质，并评估ψ(4660)衰变作为探测这类奇特态平台的可行性。

Method: 将ψ(4660)视为常规ψ(5S)粲偶素态，假设目标奇特态为强子分子态，通过中间粲介子三角形圈描述产生机制；利用夸克模型估算ψ(4660)与粲介子对的耦合常数；在统一框架下系统分析四个衰变过程，并评估不同中间圈图（SHH、THH、HHH）的贡献及对截断参数的依赖性。

Result: 预测ψ(4660)→Z_c(3900)π、Z_c(4020)π、Z_cs(3985)K、Z_2(4250)π的分支比分别约为10^{-2}、10^{-4}、10^{-3}和10^{-6}量级，且对截断参数依赖较弱；SHH中间圈图在大多数道中占主导地位。

Conclusion: ψ(4660)衰变中这些奇特态的产生率可观，为探测带电隐藏粲奇特态的分子本质及检验其产生机制提供了有前景的实验平台。

Abstract: We investigate the production of several hidden-charm exotic candidates, including $Z_c(3900)$, $Z_c(4020)$, $Z_{cs}(3985)$, and $Z_2(4250)$, in $ψ(4660)$ decays under the assumption that these states are predominantly hadronic molecules. Treating $ψ(4660)$ as a conventional $ψ(5S)$ charmonium state, the production mechanisms are described through intermediate charmed-meson triangle loops, with its couplings to charmed-meson pairs estimated within the quark model. A systematic analysis of the processes $ψ(4660)\to Z_c(3900)π$, $ψ(4660)\to Z_c(4020)π$, $ψ(4660)\to Z_{cs}(3985)K$, and $ψ(4660)\to Z_2(4250)π$ is performed within a unified framework. The predicted branching fractions are found to be of the order of $10^{-2}$, $10^{-4}$, $10^{-3}$, and $10^{-6}$, respectively, exhibiting only a mild dependence on the cutoff parameter. We further find that the contributions from the $SHH$ intermediate loops dominate over those from the $THH$ and $HHH$ loops in most channels. The sizable production rates obtained in this work indicate that $ψ(4660)$ decays provide a promising platform for probing the molecular nature of charged hidden-charm exotic states and testing their underlying production mechanisms.

</details>


### [104] [Study of $χ_{c1,2}$ decays to $PPP$ via light meson resonances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09096)
*Bo Lan,Jie Zhu,Jin-Huan Sheng,Yuan-Guo Xu,Ru-Min Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用SU(3)味对称性方法，系统研究了χ_{c1,2} → P₁P₂P₃三体衰变过程，计算了包含矢量(V)、张量(T)和标量(S)介子共振贡献的分支比，并在窄宽度近似和有限宽度效应下给出预测结果。研究发现除已观测到的χ_{c1} → π⁺π⁻η衰变中的a₀⁺(980)共振信号外，其他道道（特别是χ_{c1,2} → KKπ）中可能存在显著的V和T介子共振贡献，值得实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粲偶素三体衰变为研究非微扰QCD动力学和探索轻介子共振态提供了重要实验平台，目前实验观测到的共振信号主要集中于χ_{c1} → π⁺π⁻η衰变，其他道道的共振贡献尚待系统理论预言和实验验证。

Method: 基于SU(3)味对称性方法，分别考虑矢量(V)介子、张量(T)介子和标量(S)介子共振贡献，在窄宽度近似和有限宽度效应下计算χ_{c1,2} → P₁P₂P₃衰变的分支比，并给出χ_{c1,2} → (V/T)P₃ → P₁P₂P₃及χ_{c1} → SP₃ → P₁P₂P₃过程的预测结果。

Result: 在已实验观测到χ_{c1} → π⁺π⁻η中显著的a₀⁺(980)共振信号的基础上，预言了其他道道中潜在的共振贡献；尤其在χ_{c1,2} → KKπ道道中，预测结果显示V介子和T介子存在显著的共振贡献。

Conclusion: χ_{c1,2} → KKπ道道中的V和T介子共振贡献值得进一步的实验验证和探索，为未来相关实验研究提供了理论指导。

Abstract: The three-body decays of charmonium states provide a valuable laboratory for probing non-perturbative QCD dynamics and investigating light meson resonances. Three-body decays $χ_{c1,2} \to P_{1}P_{2}P_{3}$ are studied in this work ($P$ denotes the light pseudoscalar meson). Branching ratio analysis is performed for the $χ_{c1,2} \to P_{1}P_{2}P_{3}$ decays incorporating light vector ($V$) meson resonance, light tensor ($T$) meson resonance and light scalar ($S$) meson resonance contributions. Based on the SU(3) flavor symmetry approach, we calculate the branching ratios under both the narrow width approximation and the finite width effect, and provide the predict results in the $χ_{c1,2}\to (V/T)P_{3} \to P_{1}P_{2}P_{3}$ processes. Furthermore, based on the significant $a^{+}_{0}(980)$ resonance signal in $χ_{c1} \to π^{+ }π^{- }η$ decay, the predict results for $χ_{c1}\to SP_{3} \to P_{1}P_{2}P_{3}$ are also obtained. While the resonance signals observed in current experiments are mainly concentrated in the $χ_{c1} \to π^+ π^- η$ decay, our results suggest potential resonance contributions in other channels. Especially in $χ_{c1,2}\to KKπ$ channels, the predicted results show the significant resonance contributions from $V$ mesons and $T$ mesons, which is worth further experimental verification and exploration.

</details>


### [105] [Towards mixed QCDxEW corrections to the charged-current Drell-Yan process with leptonic decay at high transverse masses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09124)
*Christian Biello,Amlan Chakraborty,Giulio Gambuti,Raoul Roentsch,Chiara Signorile-Signorile*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文报告了在嵌套软共线相减（NSCS）框架下，计算带电电流Drell-Yan过程的混合QCD×EW NNLO修正的进展。研究验证了NLO结果，展示了NNLO实修正，并比较了中性流与新开发的带电电流通道的影响，旨在满足LHC未来对百分量级精度的需求，特别是在Sudakov对数增强的电弱效应区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Drell-Yan过程是LHC的关键精度基准和SM/SMEFT灵敏探针，充分挖掘未来LHC数据需要百分量级的SM理论预言，这要求精确的EW和混合QCD×EW修正。

Method: 在嵌套软共线相减（Nested Soft-Collinear Subtraction, NSCS）框架下，计算带电电流Drell-Yan过程（含轻子衰变）在高横质量区域的混合QCD×EW NNLO修正。

Result: 完成了NLO验证，给出了NNLO实修正结果，并比较了中性流与带电电流通道的修正效应差异。

Conclusion: 这些混合修正是匹配当前及未来LHC测量精度的关键，尤其在Sudakov对数增强的电弱效应区域不可或缺。

Abstract: The Drell-Yan process is a key precision benchmark at the Large Hadron Collider and a sensitive probe of SM and SMEFT effects. Fully exploiting future LHC data requires percent-level SM predictions, motivating precise EW and mixed QCDxEW corrections. In this proceeding we report of the progress towards the calculation of mixed QCDxEW corrections to charged-current Drell-Yan with leptonic decay at high transverse masses, performed within the Nested Soft-Collinear Subtraction framework. After presenting a validation at NLO, we show results for the NNLO real corrections and compare their impact in the neutral-current and newly developed charged-current channels. These contributions are essential to match the current and future precision of LHC measurements, especially in regions where electroweak effects are enhanced by Sudakov logarithms.

</details>


### [106] [Minimal Majoron Dark Matter from a Discrete $Z_N$ Gauge Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09131)
*Subaru Fujisawa,Qiuyue Liang,Shigeki Matsumoto,Michiru Uwabo-Niibo,Tsutomu T. Yanagida*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在最小扩展标准模型（添加三个右手中微子和一个复标量场）中，由精确离散$Z_N$规范对称性（$Z_N\subset U(1)_{B-L}$）诱导的Majoron暗物质。通过Planck-scale算符破坏偶然的全局$U(1)_{B-L}$对称性产生小质量Majoron，作为准Nambu-Goldstone玻色子暗物质候选者。研究其在辐射主导和早期物质主导宇宙学历史下的misalignment产生机制，发现$Z_5$模型因衰变到中微子被排除，而$Z_7$模型预测1-10 MeV质量范围的Majoron，可通过未来MeV伽马射线观测（如COSI）的511 keV线和双光子单能线进行探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在最小扩展标准模型中研究由离散$Z_N$对称性自然产生的Majoron暗物质，解决其质量起源问题，并寻找可观测的暗物质信号。

Method: 利用离散$Z_N$规范对称性（$Z_5,Z_7,Z_{11},Z_{13}$）将全局$U(1)_{B-L}$作为低能偶然对称性，通过Planck-suppressed算符显式破缺产生小Majoron质量；采用misalignment机制计算暗物质丰度，考虑辐射主导和早期物质主导两种宇宙学历史；结合等曲率扰动约束、宇宙学限制和间接探测对参数空间进行扫描。

Result: $Z_5$模型被排除（衰变到中微子主导）；$Z_7,Z_{11},Z_{13}$模型保持可行。$Z_7$模型预测Majoron质量在1-10 MeV范围，可通过未来COSI等MeV伽马射线望远镜探测其衰变产生的511 keV线和双光子线。

Conclusion: 离散$Z_N$对称性框架下的Majoron是可行的暗物质候选者，尤其是$Z_7$模型具有明确的MeV能段可观测信号，为未来伽马射线天文观测提供了具体目标。

Abstract: We investigate majoron dark matter in a minimal setup, where the Standard Model is extended by three right-handed neutrinos and a complex scalar field. The theory is defined by an exact discrete gauge symmetry, $Z_N\subset U(1)_{B-L}$, while the global $U(1)_{B-L}$ symmetry emerges only as an accidental symmetry at low energies. For nontrivial choices of the discrete symmetry $Z_N$, such as $Z_5$, $Z_7$, $Z_{11}$, and $Z_{13}$, Planck-suppressed operators explicitly break this accidental symmetry and generate a small majoron mass, making the resulting pseudo-Nambu--Goldstone boson a well-motivated dark matter candidate. We study its production via the misalignment mechanism after inflation, considering both radiation-dominated and early matter-dominated cosmological histories, and confront the viable parameter space with isocurvature bounds, cosmological constraints, and indirect dark matter searches. We find that the $Z_5$ model is excluded by limits on the dominant dark matter decay into neutrinos, whereas the other models remain viable. In particular, the $Z_7$ scenario predicts a majoron mass in the $1$--$10\,{\rm MeV}$ range and can be sensitively probed by future MeV gamma-ray observations, especially with COSI, through the 511$\,$keV line from the majoron decay into an electron--positron pair and the monochromatic gamma-ray line from its decay into two photons.

</details>


### [107] [Enhancing the sensitivity to FCNC top decays $t\to cH $ and $t\to cS $ in the boosted regime at CLIC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09154)
*Shuo Yang,Peng-Bo Zhao,Ji-Chong Yang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用CLIC对撞机在1.5 TeV能级下，通过构建多通道喷注图像并结合CNN分类器，在 boosted 运动学区域中研究顶夸克的FCNC衰变过程t→cH和t→cS，给出了95%置信水平下的分支比上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顶夸克具有最大的汤川耦合，是探索电弱对称性破缺和新物理的独特窗口，稀有顶夸克过程是探测新物理的有力工具。

Method: 在CLIC对撞机√s=1.5 TeV条件下，聚焦于顶夸克高度 boosted 的运动学区域，构建多通道喷注图像，利用卷积神经网络(CNN)分类器提取喷注子结构模式以增强信号鉴别能力。

Result: 在积分亮度4 ab⁻¹下，得到95% C.L.上限BR(t→cH)×BR(H→bb̅)<5.27×10⁻⁵；对于质量为30-80 GeV的标量粒子S，BR(t→cS)×BR(S→bb̅)的95% C.L.上限在3.25×10⁻⁵到5.26×10⁻⁵之间。

Conclusion: 研究证明了CNN结合喷注图像方法在 boosted 区域中对FCNC顶夸克衰变搜寻的有效性，为未来轻子对撞机上探测新物理提供了重要参考。

Abstract: The top quark, having the largest Yukawa coupling to the Higgs sector, provides a unique window into electroweak symmetry breaking and possible new physics beyond the Standard Model. Searches for rare top-quark processes are thus powerful probes of new physics. In this work, we investigate the flavor-changing neutral-current (FCNC) top-quark decays $t\to cH$ and $t\to cS$, where $S$ denotes a light scalar, at the Compact Linear Collider (CLIC) with a center-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s}=1.5~\mathrm{TeV}$. Our analysis focuses on a kinematic regime distinct from most previous studies, in which the top quarks are typically highly boosted. To enhance signal discrimination in the boosted regime, we construct multi-channel jet images and employ a convolutional neural network (CNN) classifier to capture jet-substructure patterns relevant to the FCNC signals. Assuming an integrated luminosity of $4~\mathrm{ab}^{-1}$, we obtain the expected $95\%$ C.L. upper limit $\mathrm{BR}(t\to cH)\times \mathrm{BR}(H\to b\bar b)<5.27\times10^{-5}$. For the exotic scalar singlet, expected $95\%$ C.L. upper limits between $3.25\times10^{-5}$ and $5.26\times10^{-5}$ are obtained for $\mathrm{BR}(t\to cS)\times \mathrm{BR}(S\to b\bar b)$, for scalar masses between $30$ and $80~\mathrm{GeV}$.

</details>


### [108] [Exclusive $J/ψ$ photoproduction in photon-proton diffractive scattering: A light-front Hamiltonian approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09206)
*Xiaoyi Wu,Zhi Hu,Chandan Mondal,Jiangshan Lan,Siqi Xu,Jiatong Wu,Xingbo Zhao,James P. Vary*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Basis Light-Front Quantization（BLFQ）框架下研究了光子-质子衍射散射中独占J/ψ产生的截面，利用偶电荷交换的双胶子（“坡密子”）交换机制，将振幅分解为偶极子散射振幅与J/ψ和光子光前波函数的卷积，为未来电子-离子对撞机实验提供理论指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究光子-质子衍射散射中独占J/ψ产生过程的截面，为未来电子-离子对撞机（EIC）实验提供理论基础和可检验的预测。

Method: 采用BLFQ框架，使用偶极子模型中的电荷共轭偶双胶子交换（“坡密子”）机制；将散射振幅分解为偶极子散射振幅与J/ψ和光子LFWFs的卷积；偶极子散射振幅表示为插入质子态之间的胶子场算符矩阵元，依赖于质子LFWFs和Bjorken标度变量x；质子和J/ψ的LFWFs通过对角化各自光前哈密顿量获得，虚光子LFWFs来自微扰QCD。

Result: 得到的结果可为Balitsky-Kovchegov（BK）方程提供初始条件，用于探测更小Bjorken标度下的质子结构。

Conclusion: 该工作为未来电子-离子对撞机实验提供了有价值的理论洞见。

Abstract: We investigate the cross-section for exclusive $J/ψ$ production in photon-proton diffractive scattering within the Basis Light-Front Quantization (BLFQ) framework. The leading-order contribution to this process is well approximated by the charge conjugation-even two-gluon (``pomeron") exchange mechanism in the dipole model, which factorizes the total amplitude into the dipole scattering amplitude and the convolution of the $J/ψ$ and photon light-front wave functions (LFWFs). We express the dipole scattering amplitude as the matrix element of gluon field operators inserted between proton states, with the element being sensitive to the proton LFWFs and the Bjorken scaling variable, $x$. The proton and $J/ψ$ LFWFs are obtained by diagonalizing their respective light-front Hamiltonians within the BLFQ approach, while the virtual photon LFWFs are employed from perturbative QCD. Our results provide initial conditions for the Balitsky-Kovchegov (BK) equation, which can be used to probe the proton structure at smaller Bjorken scales. This work offers valuable theoretical insights for future electron-ion collider experiments.

</details>


### [109] [Hunting for new physics in $B$ meson $b \to u$ transitions: A fresh look with Belle II data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09216)
*Pietro Colangelo,Fulvia De Fazio,Maria Letizia Di Cuia,Davide Milillo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用Belle II合作组最近报道的半轻子衰变微分分支比精确测量数据，对三种B介子衰变过程进行分析。采用包含所有左手中微子维度六算符的广义低能有效哈密顿量（符合SMEFT框架），考虑复数的、依赖轻子味的Wilson系数。针对三个代表性的|Vub|值，通过拟合q²分箱观测量约束耦合参数空间并确定置信区域，最后对三个衰变通道进行全局拟合，同时确定|Vub|和新物理耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用Belle II最新的精确测量数据，在SMEFT框架下通过广义有效哈密顿量研究B介子半轻子衰变，同时约束新物理耦合参数和CKM矩阵元|Vub|。

Method: 采用包含所有维度六左手中微子算符的广义低能有效哈密顿量（SMEFT一致），考虑复数、轻子味依赖的Wilson系数；对三个代表性|Vub|值进行q²分箱观测量拟合以约束参数空间；最后对三个衰变通道进行全局拟合联合确定|Vub|和新物理耦合。

Result: 得到了对耦合参数空间的约束及对应的置信区域，以及全局拟合下|Vub|和新物理耦合的联合确定结果。

Conclusion: Belle II精确数据可有效约束半轻子B衰变中的新物理参数空间，并在全局拟合中同时提取|Vub|。

Abstract: We present an analysis of the decays $B^- \to \ell^- {\bar ν}_\ell$, $\bar B^0 \to π^+ \ell^- {\bar ν}_\ell$, and $B^- \to ρ^0 \ell^- {\bar ν}_\ell$ leveraging the precise measurements of binned differential branching fractions recently reported by the Belle II Collaboration for the semileptonic modes. We adopt a generalized low-energy Hamiltonian that incorporates all dimension-six operators with left-handed neutrinos -- consistent with the Standard Model effective field theory (SMEFT) -- featuring complex, lepton-flavor-dependent Wilson coefficients. For three representative values of $|V_{ub}|$, we perform a fit to the measured $q^2$-binned observables to constrain the coupling parameter space and determine the corresponding confidence regions. Finally, we perform a global fit for the three decay channels, determining $|V_{ub}|$ together with the new physics couplings.

</details>


### [110] [Tensor-polarized parton distribution functions of the deuteron by a convolution model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09237)
*S. Kumano,Kenshi Kuroki*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过卷积形式计算了氘核的张量极化部分子分布函数（PDFs），发现其结果与HERMES实验测得的b1数据存在显著差异，暗示需要超越简单质子-中子束缚系统的新机制。同时将结果转换为Trento约定下的PDFs，并利用Wandzila-Wilczek类关系估计了twist-3 PDFs，为JLab和费米实验室等即将开展的实验提供了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算氘核的张量极化部分子分布函数（PDFs），并与HERMES实验数据进行对比，以检验现有卷积形式理论框架的有效性。

Method: 采用卷积形式，将核子非极化PDFs与氘核中的张量极化核子动量分布进行卷积，得到氘核的张量极化PDFs；随后利用Wandzila-Wilczek类关系估计twist-3 PDFs。

Result: 计算得到的张量极化PDFs与HERMES数据确定的结果存在显著差异，表明需要引入超越简单质子-中子束缚体系的新机制来解释这一差异。

Conclusion: 当前基于卷积形式的理论计算与实验结果不符，亟需进一步研究；同时，所得PDFs可为JLab和费米实验室等即将开展的张量极化氘靶实验提供理论参考和测试对象。

Abstract: Tensor-polarized parton distribution functions (PDFs) are calculated for the deuteron by using a convolution formalism, where the tensor-polarized PDFs are given by the corresponding nucleon's unpolarized PDFs convoluted with the tensor-polarized nucleon momentum distribution in the deuteron. These distributions are obtained at $Q^2=2.5$ GeV$^2$ in order to compare with the tensor-polarized PDFs which were determined by HERMES $b_1$ data. The obtained distributions are very different from the ones determined from the HERMES data, which indicates further studies are needed to clarify the difference, possibly by considering a new mechanism beyond the simple bound system of a proton and a neutron. The obtained PDFs $δ_T q $ and $δ_T \bar q$ are converted to the PDFs of the Trento convention $f_{1LL}^{\, q}$ and $f_{1LL}^{\, \bar q}$, and they are used for estimating the twist-3 PDFs $f_{LT}^{\, q}$ and $f_{LT}^{\, \bar q}$ by using a Wandzura-Wilczek-like relation. Because deep-inelastic-scattering experiments are under preparation for structure functions with a tensor-polarized deuteron target at the Thomas Jefferson National Accelerator Facility, and a Drell-Yan experiment will be possible at hadron accelerator facilities, such as the Fermi National Accelerator Laboratory, the obtained tensor-polarized PDFs will be tested experimentally.

</details>


### [111] [Leptogenesis with sub-electroweak-scale reheating temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09282)
*Basabendu Barman,Arindam Basu,Debasish Borah,Nayan Das*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了再加热温度低于球谐函数冻结温度时，通过轻子生成机制产生宇宙重子不对称性的过程。在单幂律膨胀势框架下，分析了三种微扰再加热情景，确定了能成功再现观测到的重子不对称性的参数空间，并强调了未来原始引力波观测对这一类情景的探测潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索再加热温度低于球谐函数冻结温度时，通过轻子生成机制产生宇宙重子不对称性的可行性，填补现有研究空白。

Method: 在单幂律膨胀势框架下，分析三种微扰再加热情景：膨胀子衰变为SM类玻色子对、SM类费米子对、或重右手中微子对。通过追踪再加热期间的球谐函数相互作用率，确定符合观测重子不对称性的参数空间。

Result: 识别出每种情景下成功再现观测重子不对称性的参数空间区域，并满足现有宇宙学约束。

Conclusion: 该轻子生成情景具有可行性，未来原始引力波观测可用于探测这类模型。

Abstract: We study the generation of the baryon asymmetry of the Universe via leptogenesis during the post-inflationary reheating epoch, considering reheating temperatures below the temperature of sphaleron freeze-out temperature. Within the framework of a monomial inflaton potential during reheating, we analyze three perturbative reheating scenarios in which the inflaton decays into (i) a pair of SM-like bosons, (ii) a pair of SM-like fermions, or (iii) exclusively into a pair of heavy right-handed neutrinos. For each case, we identify the regions of parameter space that successfully reproduce the observed baryon asymmetry consistently tracking the sphaleron interaction rate during reheating, while satisfying existing cosmological constraints. We also highlight the potential of future primordial gravitational wave observations to probe this class of scenarios.

</details>


### [112] [Diffusion Monte Carlo study of deuteron-like fully light hexaquarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09288)
*M. C. Gordillo,J. Segovia*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 利用扩散蒙特卡洛方法在组分夸克模型中研究全轻六夸克态（三u夸克和三d夸克），发现紧凑六夸克构型能量远高于重子-重子阈值，而类双重子构型能量接近但高于NN、NΔ和ΔΔ阈值，其中一种构型呈现类似氘核的空间结构但能量略高于阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索轻夸克扇区中是否存在深束缚的紧凑六夸克态或分子态的类双重子系统，检验组分夸克模型对全轻六夸克态的描述能力。

Method: 采用扩散蒙特卡洛（Diffusion Monte Carlo）方法，在组分夸克模型框架下分别对紧凑六夸克构型和重子-重子类构型进行数值模拟计算。

Result: 所有紧凑六夸克构型能量均远高于对应的重子-重子阈值；多个类双重子构型能量接近但高于NN、NΔ和ΔΔ阈值；一种构型呈现两个间隔数飞米的类核子子团簇，空间结构与氘核高度相似，但能量仍略高于对应阈值。

Conclusion: 在该组分夸克模型框架下，轻夸克扇区不利于形成深束缚的紧凑六夸克态；类双重子构型可能形成分子态，但尚未出现真正的束缚态，需进一步研究以确认是否存在弱束缚的六夸克分子态。

Abstract: We perform a Diffusion Monte Carlo study of fully light hexaquark containing three $u$ quarks and three $d$ quarks within a constituent-quark model. Both compact and baryon--baryon-like arrangements were considered separately. All compact hexaquark configurations are found well above their corresponding baryon--baryon thresholds, suggesting that deeply bound compact six-quark states are not favored in the light-quark sector within this model. By contrast, several dibaryon-like configurations lie close, but above, to the $NN$, $NΔ$, and $ΔΔ$ thresholds and show spatial structures compatible with molecular states. One configuration exhibits two well-defined nucleon-like subclusters separated by several femtometers, closely resembling the spatial structure of the deuteron, although its calculated energy remains slightly above the corresponding threshold.

</details>


### [113] [Running coupling effects in the anti-collinear resummation in high energy evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09337)
*Alex Kovner,Michael Lublinsky,Maxim Nefedov,Vladimir Skokov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究QCD耦合运行对JIMWLK演化中反共线重求和的影响，发现耦合运行进一步减缓BFKL演化，但广义特征函数在γ=1处的值因微妙抵消而不受影响。反共线区间的Pomeron截距被重求和与耦合运行显著压低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究QCD耦合常数运行效应对JIMWLK方程中反共线重求和的影响，明确进入JIMWLK核的耦合常数标度选择，并推导包含运行耦合效应的反共线重求和BFKL核。

Method: 通过确定JIMWLK核中耦合常数的合适标度选择，在重求和方程（DGLAP）和JIMWLK核本身中同时纳入运行耦合效应，推导反共线重求和的BFKL核。开发近似方法利用广义特征函数研究BFKL格林函数。

Result: 耦合运行进一步减缓BFKL演化；广义特征函数在γ=1处因微妙抵消而不受运行耦合影响；反共线区间的Pomeron截距被重求和与运行耦合显著压低。

Conclusion: 运行耦合与反共线重求和的联合效应显著降低了Pomeron截距，对理解高能QCD演化具有重要意义。

Abstract: We study the effects of the running of the QCD coupling on the anti-collinear resummation in JIMWLK evolution in the linear (BFKL) regime. We determine the appropriate scale choice for the coupling entering the JIMWLK kernel, and derive the anti-collinearly resummed BFKL kernel, which includes running-coupling effects both in the resummation equation (i.e. DGLAP) and in the JIMWLK kernel proper. We find that the running of the coupling generally further slows down BFKL evolution, as expected. Surprisingly however the value of the generalized characteristic function at $γ=1$ is unaffected by the running coupling owing to subtle cancellations. We develop an approximation that allows us to use the generalized characteristic function to study the BFKL Green's function. Within this approximation we find that the Pomeron intercept in the anti-collinear regime is significantly reduced by both, the resummation and the running of the coupling.

</details>


### [114] [A Window onto New Invisible Particles via Semi-Visible Higgs Decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09344)
*Sally Dawson,Arnab Roy,German Valencia*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究LHC上通过半可见希格斯衰变模式H→ℓ⁺ℓ⁻+E̸_T和H→jj+E̸_T搜索新不可见粒子的参数空间覆盖，利用维数六有效场论参数化新粒子与SM的相互作用，并通过基于cut分析和多元BDT方法进行信号提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LHC上通过多种形式（新粒子搜索、SM耦合精密测量、不可见粒子信号搜索）寻找新物理，本研究聚焦于利用半可见希格斯衰变模式探索不可见新粒子的参数空间。

Method: 利用维数六有效场论参数化新不可见粒子及其与SM的相互作用；分别研究H→ℓ⁺ℓ⁻+E̸_T和H→jj+E̸_T信号及对应本底；首先使用基于cut的分析方法，随后使用多元BDT（Boosted Decision Tree）进行信号提取。

Result: 信号较小而本底较大，但过程的运动学足够丰富，使得信号提取成为可能。

Conclusion: 半可见希格斯衰变模式的运动学特征足以支持通过cut-based分析和BDT多元分析方法实现信号提取，从而探索新不可见粒子的参数空间。

Abstract: Searches for new physics continue at the LHC in several forms, including new-particle searches, precision measurements of SM couplings, and searches for signals of new invisible particles. In this talk, we discuss the reach in parameter space of new invisible particles with the semi-visible Higgs decay modes $H\to \ell^+\ell^- + ~\rm E{\!\!\!/}_T$ and $H\to jj + ~\rm E{\!\!\!/}_T$. We first parametrise the new invisible particles and their interactions with the SM through an effective field theory at dimension six. We then study the respective signals and the corresponding background, finding small signals with large backgrounds. We find, however, that the kinematics of these processes are sufficiently rich to allow a signal extraction that we first quantify with a cut-based analysis and later with a multivariate BDT.

</details>


### [115] [Fully-beauty tensor tetraquark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09354)
*S. S. Agaev,K. Azizi,H. Sundu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用QCD求和规则方法计算了全美四夸克态T=bb\bar{b}\bar{b}的质量和耦合常数，预测质量为(18530±86) MeV，总宽度为(48±6) MeV。该态对η_b η_b和ΥΥ介子对衰变稳定，但可通过\bar{b}b湮灭为轻夸克后衰变为B介子对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索全重四夸克态（特别是全美四夸克态）的存在性与性质，为实验发现全重共振态提供理论依据。

Method: 采用QCD求和规则方法：两点求和规则计算质量和耦合常数，三点求和规则计算四夸克-介子-介子顶点处的强耦合常数，从而得到各衰变道的部分宽度。

Result: 质量m=(18530±86) MeV，总宽度Γ=(48±6) MeV。该态对η_b η_b和ΥΥ衰变稳定，但可通过\bar{b}b湮灭为轻夸克后衰变为B^{(*)+}B^{(*)-}、B^{(*)0}\bar{B}^{(*)0}和B_s^{(*)0}\bar{B}_s^{(*)0}介子对。

Conclusion: 全美四夸克态T=bb\bar{b}\bar{b}是一个相对窄的共振态，其质量和宽度的理论预测对实验寻找全重强子共振态具有参考价值。

Abstract: Parameters of the fully-beauty tensor tetraquark $ T=bb\overline{b}\overline{b}$ are computed using QCD sum rule method. The mass $m$ and coupling $Λ$ of this state are evaluated by means of two-point sum rule approach. Prediction $m=(18530 \pm 86)~\mathrm{MeV}$ for the mass demonstrates that $T$ is stable against decays to pairs of $η_b η_b$ and $ΥΥ$ mesons. But it transforms to conventional particles due to $\overline{b}b$ annihilations to light quarks $\overline{q} q $ and $\overline{s}s$ followed by creation of mesons $B^{(\ast)+}B^{( \ast)-}$, $B^{(\ast)0}\overline{B}^{(\ast)0}$ and $B_s^{(\ast)0}\overline{B} _s^{(\ast)0}$. Partial width of these decay channels are calculated by invoking technical tools of three-point sum rule method. The latter allows us to estimate strong couplings at relevant tetraquark-meson-meson vertices and, hence, a width of the process under consideration. Our prediction $ Γ=(48 \pm 6)~\mathrm{MeV}$ along with the mass of this state can be valuable for experimental studies of all-heavy resonances.

</details>


### [116] [Analysis of the hidden-charm pentaquark candidates in the $J/ψΩ$ mass spectrum via the QCD sum rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09430)
*Zhi-Gang Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过QCD求和规则详细研究了$sssc\bar{c}$五夸克态（二夸克-二夸克-反夸克类型）的质量谱，并建议在$Ω_b^- \to P_{csss}^-φ\to J/ψΩ^- φ$衰变中寻找这些奇特态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索二夸克-二夸克-反夸克类型的$sssc\bar{c}$五夸克态的质量谱，为区分五夸克态与介子-重子分子态提供实验判据。

Method: 采用QCD求和规则方法，对同位旋-自旋-宇称$IJ^{P}=0{\frac{1}{2}}^-$、$0{\frac{3}{2}}^-$和$0{\frac{5}{2}}^-$的三个五夸克态进行系统分析，提取基态质量谱。

Result: 获得了三个量子数通道下的基态质量谱数值结果。

Conclusion: 建议在$Ω_b^- \to P_{csss}^-φ\to J/ψΩ^- φ$衰变过程中寻找这些奇特态，其观测有助于区分二夸克-二夸克-反夸克型五夸克态与介子-重子型分子态。

Abstract: We explore the diquark-diquark-antiquark type $sssc\bar{c}$ pentaquark states with the isospin-spin-parity $IJ^{P}=0{\frac{1}{2}}^-$, $0{\frac{3}{2}}^-$ and $0{\frac{5}{2}}^-$ via the QCD sum rules in details and obtain the ground state mass spectrum. And we suggest to hunt for these exotic states in the exclusive decay $Ω_b^- \to P_{csss}^-φ\to J/ψΩ^- φ$. Observations of the $P_{csss}$ states would shed light on the nature of exotic states and provide an excellent opportunity to distinguish the diquark-diquark-antiquark type pentaquark states and meson-baryon type molecular states.

</details>


### [117] [Disentangling short- vs. long-distance dynamics in $B\to K^{*}μ^+μ^-$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09458)
*Marzia Bordone,Gino Isidori,Arianna Tinari*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用LHCb最新的全角度分布测量数据，通过色散关系描述非局域贡献，对$B\to K^{*}μ^+μ^-$过程中的短程与长程动力学进行了更新分析。结果表明，在数据约束下提取的短距离系数$C_9$虽低于标准模型预言，但偏差未超过$2σ$，且无需引入额外强子参数即可良好描述现有数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用LHCb最新测量的完整角分布数据，结合色散描述方法，约束长距离非局域贡献，从而更精确地提取短距离物理信息，检验标准模型。

Method: 基于LHCb对$B\to K^{*}μ^+μ^-$过程全角分布的测量数据，利用此前发表的全局振幅拟合，采用色散描述来参数化非局域贡献，通过振幅拟合确定自由参数，从而区分短程与长程贡献。

Result: 色散模型在不引入额外强子参数的情况下良好描述了数据；短距离系数$C_9$低于标准模型预言但更接近；整体张力不超过$2σ$；获得了角观测量$S_{7,8,9}$的精确后测。

Conclusion: 色散描述方法能有效约束长距离贡献，使短距离提取更可靠；未来更精确的数据可进一步检验非局域效应的描述，并实现更稳健的短距离信息提取。

Abstract: We present an updated data-driven analysis of short- vs. long-distance dynamics in $B\to K^{*}μ^+μ^-$. The analysis is based on the recent LHCb measurement of the full angular distribution of this process, taking advantage of the previously published global amplitude fit. Thanks to the precise di-lepton invariant-mass binning and angular observables directly sensitive to absorptive phases, current data provide novel insights into non-local contributions. We analyze the latter using a dispersive description, whose free parameters are determined via the amplitude fit. The dispersive model provides a satisfactory overall description of current data, without the need to introduce additional ad hoc hadronic parameters. The extracted value of the short-distance coefficient $C_9$ is lower than its Standard Model prediction, but significantly closer to it than in analyses where long-distance contributions are not constrained by data. The overall tension with the SM does not exceed the $2σ$ level. We also obtain precise postdictions for the angular observables $S_{7,8,9}$, and envisage novel consistency tests among them. In view of future, more precise data, these could provide valuable tests of the description of non-local effects in this process that, if further validated, would enable an even more robust extraction of short-distance information.

</details>


### [118] [Top-Antitop Production and Decay at Threshold at the LHC in QCD Perturbation Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09539)
*Paolo Nason,Giovanni Pelliccioli,Emanuele Re,Luca Rottoli*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对LHC上top-antitop对在阈值附近（质量接近两倍top pole质量）的产生过程，构建了三种包含阈值增强效应的NLO+PS发生器，研究了非相对论效应、toponium贡献以及top有限宽度对阈值增强修正的影响，这些发生器与ATLAS和CMS在赝标量道中观测到的阈值增强tƫ产生相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LHC上top-antitop对产生过程中，当质量接近阈值时会出现增强的微扰修正，现有NLO和NNLO结果未能完全包含这些效应；同时实验上ATLAS和CMS已观测到赝标量道中阈值附近的tƫ产生增强现象，需要理论工具来精确描述这些非相对论效应。

Method: 构建了三种NLO+PS（Next-to-Leading-Order可接驳部分子簇射）发生器，这些发生器包含了可被计算至所有微扰阶数的阈值增强修正；通过对比研究非相对论增强效应的大小、可归因于toponium产生的贡献比例，以及top有限宽度对阈值增强修正的影响。

Result: 量化了未包含在已知NLO和NNLO微扰结果中的非相对论增强效应的大小；评估了可粗略归因于toponium产生的贡献比例；揭示了top有限宽度对阈值增强修正的影响程度。

Conclusion: 所构建的发生器能够有效描述LHC上top-antitop在阈值附近的增强产生现象，对于理解ATLAS和CMS实验在赝标量道中观测到的增强tƫ产生具有重要意义。

Abstract: In this work we consider the production of a top-antitop pair at the LHC when the mass of the pair is relatively near to the nominal threshold, that is to say to twice the top pole mass. In this regime, enhanced perturbative corrections arise that can be computed to all orders in perturbation theory. We present three generators of the NLO+PS kind (Next-to-Leading-Order that can be interfaced to parton showers) that include these threshold enhanced effects. Using these generators we address the following questions: what is the size of enhanced non-relativistic effects that are not already present in the well known NLO and NNLO perturbative results; what is the size of the contribution from these effects that can be loosely attributed to toponium production; and to what extent the finite width of the top quark affects threshold enhanced corrections. Our generators are relevant for the recent observation of enhanced $t{\bar t}$ production near threshold in the pseudoscalar channel by the ATLAS and CMS collaborations.

</details>


### [119] [SCET sum rules for $B\to D_1(2420)$ and $B\to D_1'(2430)$ form factors at next-to-leading order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09573)
*Jun-Wei Zhang,Yong-Kang Huang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次在软共线有效理论(SCET)框架下，利用光锥求和规则计算了B→D₁(2420)和B→D₁'(2430)跃迁形状因子至O(αₛ)阶。通过匹配QCD跃迁流到SCET_I，并分解SCET_II中的真空到B介子关联函数，构建了有效SCET形状因子的领导功率求和规则。特别计算了由有限粲夸克质量诱导的额外纵向形状因子，其贡献仅依赖于B介子光锥分布振幅。通过引入插值流组合来区分混合的D₁和D₁'态，并扣除基态污染。利用BCL参数化将物理形状因子的q²依赖性外推到整个运动学区域。最终给出分支比、微分衰变宽度和轻子普适性比值的唯象预言，数值上得到R(D₁)=0.070⁺⁰·⁰²⁸₋₀.₀₁₈和R(D₁')=0.159⁺⁰·⁰³²₋₀.₀₂₅，可供Belle II和LHCb未来实验检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B→D₁和B→D₁'跃迁形状因子是理解B介子半轻衰变中轨道激发粲介子产生的关键输入，但此前缺乏精确的理论计算。这些形状因子对于检验轻子普适性、寻找新物理信号至关重要，尤其是实验上已观测到R(D)和R(D*)与标准模型预言的偏差，需要精确计算其他粲介子通道以辅助理解。

Method: 采用SCET框架下的光锥求和规则方法。首先将QCD跃迁流匹配到SCET_I，然后在SCET_II中分解真空到B介子关联函数。通过构建插值流组合区分D₁和D₁'混合态，利用运动方程确定衰变常数。扣除基态贡献以保证求和规则稳定性。使用BCL参数化将形状因子外推到完整q²区域。

Result: 成功计算了B→D₁和B→D₁'的有效SCET形状因子ξ^R_{∥/⊥}和Ξ^R_{∥/⊥}（R=D₁,D₁'），包括有限粲夸克质量诱导的额外纵向形状因子。得到R(D₁)=0.070⁺⁰·⁰²⁸₋₀.₀₁₈和R(D₁')=0.159⁺⁰·⁰³²₋₀.₀₂₅。

Conclusion: 首次提供了B→D₁和B→D₁'跃迁形状因子的精确理论计算，给出了分支比和轻子普适性比值的唯象预言。R(D₁)和R(D₁')的数值结果可供Belle II和LHCb实验检验，对理解B介子半轻衰变中的新物理效应具有重要意义。

Abstract: We present the first calculation of the $B \to D_1(2420)$ and $B \to D'_1(2430)$ transition form factors at ${\cal O}(α_s)$ using light-cone sum rules within the framework of soft-collinear effective theory (SCET). We first match the QCD transition currents onto ${\rm SCET_I}$ and then factorize the corresponding vacuum-to-$B$-meson correlation functions in ${\rm SCET_{II}}$. The resulting factorization formulae are used to construct the leading-power sum rules for the effective SCET form factors $ξ^R_{\parallel/\perp}$ and $Ξ^R_{\parallel/\perp}$ ($R=D_1,D'_1$). In particular, we calculate the additional longitudinal form factor $ξ^R_{\parallel,m_c}$ induced by the finite charm-quark mass, whose contribution depends only on the $B$-meson light-cone distribution amplitude $φ_B^+(ω,μ)$. To disentangle the mixed $D_1$ and $D'_1$ states, we introduce dedicated combinations of interpolating currents, with their decay constants determined via the equations of motion. To isolate the orbitally excited states from ground-state contamination, we subtract the ground-state contribution from the total sum rules and examine the stability of the resulting sum rules. Furthermore, the $q^2$-dependence of the physical form factors is extrapolated over the full kinematic region using the Bourrely--Caprini--Lellouch parameterization. Finally, we provide phenomenological predictions for the branching fractions, differential decay widths, and lepton flavor universality ratios. Numerically, we obtain $R(D_1) = 0.070^{+0.028}_{-0.018}$ and $R(D'_1) = 0.159^{+0.032}_{-0.025}$, which can be confronted with the future measurements at Belle~II and LHCb.

</details>


### [120] [Second-Order Perturbative Correction for Neutrino Oscillation Tomography of the Earth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09591)
*Dmitry Zhuridov,Wiktor Porwoł*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用中微子振荡中的物质效应对地球进行断层扫描成像，通过微扰理论二阶近似计算振荡概率，为现有地震学模型提供独立约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球内部超过现有直接探测极限（仅半径的1/500）的区域仍未知，地震学和地球物理观测在预测温度、压力和化学成分分布上存在歧义，需要独立信息来改进现有地震学模型。

Method: 在微扰理论的二阶近似下推导中微子振荡概率，适用于沿中微子路径非对称的势场，并实现多层壳演化方案以更好地处理地球各层之间的密度跃变。

Result: 获得了二阶微扰理论下的相关中微子振荡概率，该结果对非对称中微子势有效，能够支持多层壳演化方案，更精确地反映地球各层密度跃变。

Conclusion: 精确计算中微子振荡中的物质效应是实现中微子振荡层析成像的必要条件，结合KM3NeT、DUNE和Hyper-Kamiokande等实验能力的提升，该方法有望为地球内部结构提供独立约束。

Abstract: The Earth's interior at depths exceeding present direct probing limit, which is just one five-hundredth of its radius, remains unknown to a significant extent due to existing ambiguities in predicting the distribution of temperature, pressure and chemical composition on the basis of seismic and geophysical observations. Scanning the Earth with neutrinos can provide independent information for refinement of existing seismological models. However, neutrinos interact with medium extremely weakly. Only tiny TeV-energy component of the atmospheric neutrino flux is noticeably absorbed while traversing the Earth, which provides limited statistical data. Flux reduction for much more common neutrinos with energies of the order of 1 GeV is vanishing. However, on their way through the planet they undergo sizable flavor oscillations that depend on the electron number density, which provides a link to seismological models. Accurate calculation of the matter effect in these oscillations is required for successful neutrino oscillation tomography of the Earth besides growing power of neutrino experiments, e. g., KM3NeT, DUNE and Hyper-Kamiokande. We obtained relevant neutrino oscillation probabilities in the second order of perturbation theory. Our results are valid for a neutrino potential that is not necessarily symmetric along the neutrino path. This allowed us to implement a multi-shell evolution scheme to better account for the density jumps between various layers of the Earth.

</details>

*Luca Dal Molin,Frank Neumann*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了I型实代数曲线上固定秩与度的实向量丛的模栈，并利用复Atiyah-Bott类诱导的特征类确定了其模2上同调代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解实代数曲线上实向量丛模栈的上同调结构，推广Atiyah-Bott经典理论到实情形。

Method: 利用复Atiyah-Bott类诱导的特征类，通过模2上同调分析研究实向量丛模栈的代数结构。

Result: 确定了I型实代数曲线上固定秩与度的实向量丛模栈的模2上同调代数。

Conclusion: 实向量丛模栈的上同调可由复Atiyah-Bott类诱导的特征类完全描述。

Abstract: We study the moduli stacks of real vector bundles of fixed rank and degree on a type I real algebraic curve and determine its mod $2$ cohomology algebra in terms of characteristic classes induced from the complex Atiyah-Bott classes.

</details>


### [122] [Hilbert schemes of points on canonical surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08913)
*Alastair Craw,Ryo Yamagishi*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了带有典范奇点的曲面S上的n点Hilbert概形，推广了Fogarty定理：$\operatorname{Hilb}^n(S)$的底层约化子概形是维数为2n的具有典范奇点的正规簇；当n≤7时，$\operatorname{Hilb}^n(S)$是约化的。在S具有辛奇点（复数域上）时，证明了其底层约化子概形也具有辛奇点，推广了Beauville的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Fogarty关于光滑曲面Hilbert概形的经典定理推广到带有典范奇点的曲面上，并进一步推广Beauville关于辛奇点的结果，建立奇点曲面与Nakajima quiver variety之间的联系。

Method: 利用底层约化子概形的结构分析，结合第一作者与Gyenge、Gammelgaard、Szendrői关于Kleinian奇点的工作，将Hilbert概形与Nakajima quiver variety建立联系。

Result: 1) $\operatorname{Hilb}^n(S)$的底层约化子概形是维数2n、具有典范奇点的正规簇；2) 当n≤7时，$\operatorname{Hilb}^n(S)$是约化的；3) 在复数域上S具有辛奇点时，$\operatorname{Hilb}^n(S)$的底层约化子概形也具有辛奇点。

Conclusion: 成功将光滑曲面Hilbert概形的正则性结果推广到典范奇点曲面，并建立了辛奇点在Hilbert概形下的保持性。

Abstract: For $n\geq 1$, we investigate the Hilbert scheme of $n$-points on a surface $S$ with canonical singularities. We generalise the well-known theorem of Fogarty by showing that the underlying reduced subscheme of $\operatorname{Hilb}^n(S)$ is a normal variety of dimension $2n$ with canonical singularities, and for $n\leq 7$, we show that $\operatorname{Hilb}^n(S)$ is reduced. When $S$ has symplectic singularities over $\mathbb{C}$, we show that the underlying reduced subscheme of $\operatorname{Hilb}^n(S)$ also has symplectic singularities, thereby generalising a result of Beauville. Our results build on work of the first author with Gyenge, Gammelgaard and Szendrői that sought to identify the underlying reduced subscheme of the Hilbert scheme of $n$-points on a Kleinian singularity with a Nakajima quiver variety.

</details>


### [123] [Asymptotic Cohomological Functions and Volumes on Proper Schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09021)
*Wenqi Li*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了域上真方案的渐近上同调函数的性质，证明了在由有界导出范畴中对象扰动下的行为公式，并利用相同方法证明了任何真方案上线丛的体积极限的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了深入理解域上真方案的渐近上同调函数的性质，特别是其在扰动下的行为模式，以及验证线丛体积作为极限存在的普遍性。

Method: 采用有界导出范畴中的对象作为扰动工具，通过分析渐近上同调函数在该扰动下的变化规律，并利用相同方法研究线丛体积的极限存在性。

Result: 得到了渐近上同调函数在扰动下的行为公式，并证明了任何真方案上线丛的体积始终作为极限存在。

Conclusion: 该研究推广并统一了渐近上同调函数和线丛体积的相关理论，为代数几何中的渐近性质提供了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: We study the behavior of asymptotic cohomological functions on proper schemes over a field. We prove a formula for how asymptotic cohomological functions behave under perturbation by an object in the bounded derived category. Using the same method, we also prove that the volume of a line bundle on any proper scheme over a field always exists as a limit.

</details>


### [124] [On the Jung-van der Kulk decomposition into Pascal finite factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09340)
*Elżbieta Adamus,Zbigniew Hajto*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文结合Jung-van der Kulk定理与Pascal有限类的共轭不变性，证明了任意满足F(0)=0的二维多项式自同构F可分解为F = diag(det J_F, 1) ∘ P₁ ∘ … ∘ P_s，其中每个P_i是Pascal有限自同构。由于Pascal有限自同构的雅可比行列式为1，对角线因子是唯一障碍：F可分解为Pascal有限映射当且仅当det J_F = 1。特别地，该结果在二维任意特征下肯定了cite{ABCH2}中的问题3.1，构成了正特征下指数生成子猜想的类比。在特征p下，每个因子的阶整除p²。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究任意域上二维多项式自同构的分解结构，探讨Pascal有限自同构作为基本构造块的可能性，并回答正特征下指数生成子猜想的类比问题。

Method: 结合Jung-van der Kulk定理（刻画二维多项式自同构群）与Pascal有限类的共轭不变性，通过代数分解方法将一般自同构表示为对角线因子与Pascal有限自同构的复合。

Result: 任意满足F(0)=0的二维多项式自同构可分解为F = diag(det J_F, 1) ∘ P₁ ∘ … ∘ P_s，其中P_i为Pascal有限自同构；F可完全由Pascal有限映射复合得到当且仅当det J_F = 1。在特征p下，因子阶整除p²。

Conclusion: 该分解定理给出了二维情形下Pascal有限自同构生成整个多项式自同构群的充要条件（行列式为1），并正面对答了相关猜想，为正特征下类似指数生成子猜想提供了二维证据。

Abstract: Combining the Jung--van der Kulk theorem with the conjugacy invariance of the Pascal finite class, we show that every polynomial automorphism $F$ of the plane over an arbitrary field $K$, satisfying $F(0) = 0$, decomposes into the form $F = \diag(\det J_F, 1) \circ P_1 \circ \dots \circ P_s$, where all $P_i$ are Pascal finite automorphisms. Since every Pascal finite automorphism has Jacobian determinant equal to 1, the diagonal factor is the only obstacle: $F$ is a composition of Pascal finite maps if and only if $\det J_F = 1$. In particular, Question~3.1 from \cite{ABCH2} has a positive answer in dimension 2 in any characteristic, which constitutes an analogue of the Exponential Generators Conjecture in positive characteristic. In characteristic $p$, the factors can be chosen to have an order dividing $p^2$.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为箭图子簇 $A_n\dots A_1=0$ 的最高维分量提供一个初等证明，避免使用复杂的几何或代数工具。

Method: 采用初等证明方法，不使用高级代数几何或表示论工具，直接分析箭图子簇的最高维分量的对称性。

Result: 给出了箭图子簇 $A_n\dots A_1=0$ 最高维分量对称性的初等证明。

Conclusion: 本文通过初等方法成功证明了所研究对象的对称性质，为理解此类结构提供了更基础的途径。

Abstract: We provide an elementary proof of a symmetric property of the top dimensional components of the quiver subvariety $A_n\dots A_1=0$.

</details>


### [126] [Inaccessibility of the flat topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09440)
*Ko Aoki,Robert Burklund*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明了平拓扑中单点覆盖在κ-cofiltered极限下不封闭，因此每个可达平层都存在更细的拓扑使其仍为层，这与弧拓扑和纯拓扑形成对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究平拓扑（flat topology）中单点覆盖在κ-cofiltered极限下的封闭性，以及这一性质对可达平层（accessible flat sheaf）的影响。

Method: 通过证明平拓扑上的单点覆盖在任意正则基数κ的κ-cofiltered极限下不封闭，来论证存在更细的拓扑使得每个可达平层仍为层。

Result: 平拓扑中单点覆盖不封闭于κ-cofiltered极限；每个可达平层都存在严格更细的拓扑使其保持为层。

Conclusion: 平拓扑在单点覆盖的极限封闭性方面与弧拓扑、纯拓扑等大拓扑存在本质差异。

Abstract: We prove that singleton covers in the flat topology on affine schemes are not closed under $κ$-cofiltered limits for any regular cardinal $κ$. Therefore, for every accessible flat sheaf there exists a strictly finer topology for which it is still a sheaf. The flat topology thus contrasts with other big topologies, such as the arc and pure topologies.

</details>


### [127] [Formal Abel relations for curves in characteristic $p$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09471)
*John B. Little*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了约化平面曲线广义雅可比簇中Abel关系的形式，从形式幂级数角度探讨特征p代数闭域上的情况，最终目标是完成作者1980年博士论文工作，建立Abel定理逆定理的一般特征p形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完成作者1980年博士论文中未竟的工作，在特征p代数闭域上建立Abel定理逆定理的一般形式，该定理与Griffiths讨论的Abel定理逆定理相关，并应用于双平移流形和Web几何中的Lie-Wirtinger定理。

Method: 采用形式幂级数观点，在约化平面曲线的广义雅可比簇中研究Abel关系的形式。

Result: 本文为工作论文，报告了近期在特征p代数闭域上约化平面曲线Abel关系形式的研究进展，但最终结果将在后续论文中完成。

Conclusion: 本文为后续建立Abel定理逆定理的一般特征p形式奠定基础，该结果将完善作者1980年博士论文的工作，并对双平移流形和Web几何有重要意义。

Abstract: In this working paper, we report recent work studying the form of Abel relations in the (generalized) Jacobians of reduced plane curves over an algebraically closed field of characteristic $p$ from a formal power series point of view. The ultimate goal (to be addressed in a subsequent paper) is to complete the work done in the author's PhD thesis from 1980 and to establish a general characteristic $p$ form of the converse of Abel's theorem discussed by Griffths and used in the Lie-Wirtinger theorem on double translation manifolds and the geometry of webs.

</details>


### [128] [Correspondences for hyperkähler varieties with large Picard numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09622)
*Ljudmila Kamenova,Abhinav Kumar*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文探索了具有大皮卡德数的超凯勒流形与阿贝尔簇之间的联系，并将Morrison关于K3曲面的定理推广到超凯勒流形的已知例子（如K3表面上的点状希尔伯特概形）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解具有大皮卡德数的超凯勒流形是否必然通过代数对应与阿贝尔簇相关联，推广Morrison对K3曲面的经典结果到更一般的超凯勒流形情形。

Method: 采用代数几何与数论方法，建立超凯勒流形与阿贝尔簇之间的代数对应关系，利用皮卡德数的条件进行分析。

Result: 成功将Morrison关于K3曲面的定理推广到已知的超凯勒流形例子，如K3表面上的点状希尔伯特概形。

Conclusion: 证明了对于特定类型的超凯勒流形，当其皮卡德数足够大时，确实可以通过代数对应与阿贝尔簇建立联系，验证了Morrison猜想在更广框架下的适用性。

Abstract: In this note, we explore the connection between hyperkähler manifolds with large Picard numbers and abelian varieties. In particular, we are interested in Morrison's solution to the (modified) Oda's conjecture: every K3 surface whose Picard group is large enough (in a certain precise sense) must be related via an algebraic correspondence to an abelian surface. We generalize this theorem to the case of known examples of hyperkähler manifolds such as pointed Hilbert schemes on K3 surfaces.

</details>

### [129] [Asymptotic stability of solutions to the good Boussinesq equation in dispersive wave region](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09050)
*Yingmin Yang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究good Boussinesq方程在色散波区域解的渐近稳定性，通过Dbar-最速下降法求解Riemann-Hilbert问题，获得了带有最优误差阶$\mathcal{O}(t^{-3/4})$的长时渐近展开式，并将初值条件从快速衰减的Schwartz空间推广到加权Sobolev空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将good Boussinesq方程解的渐近分析从快速衰减的Schwartz空间初值推广到更一般的加权Sobolev空间初值，并证明在色散波区域解的渐近稳定性。

Method: 采用Dbar-最速下降法（Dbar-steepest descent method）求解Riemann-Hilbert问题，对反射系数属于加权Sobolev空间的初值进行长时渐近分析。

Result: 获得了good Boussinesq方程解的长时渐近展开式，误差阶达到最优的$\mathcal{O}(t^{-3/4})$；将初值条件从Schwartz空间推广到加权Sobolev空间。

Conclusion: 证明了good Boussinesq方程在色散波区域解的渐近稳定性，并扩展了初值条件的适用范围。

Abstract: This work studies the asymptotic stability of solutions to the good Boussinesq equation in dispersive wave region when the reflection coefficients associated with the initial data belong to weighted Sobolev space. The Dbar-steepest descent method is applied to the Riemann-Hilbert problem and the long-time asymptotic expansion of the solution are obtained up to an optimal error of order $\mathcal{O}(t^{-3/4})$. Compared with previous results, we extend the initial data from the rapidly decaying Schwartz space to a weighted Sobolev space, and prove the asymptotic stability of the solution in dispersive wave region.

</details>


### [130] [TokaGrad: End-to-end differentiable tokamak simulator for L-to-H full scenario optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09088)
*Jaemin Seo*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了TokaGrad，一个端到端可微分的托卡马克输运模拟器，能够自洽地建模完整放电场景（包括爬升、L模和H模），并通过梯度优化实现反应堆设计、致动器控制和全场景波形优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统聚变优化依赖代价高昂的试错或暴力参数搜索，而可微编程范式能将整个模拟管道表示为可微计算图，从而直接利用模拟器内部敏感性进行梯度优化，填补了可微托卡马克模拟器在自洽全场景建模方面的空白。

Method: 构建端到端可微分的托卡马克输运模拟器TokaGrad，将等离子体平衡、输运、加热、L-H转换和台基形成等模型集成为可微操作，使致动器、等离子体响应和平衡状态通过计算图相连。

Result: 与梯度优化器耦合后，TokaGrad成功实现了反应堆设计优化、致动器控制以及全场景波形优化，证明了可微模拟框架在托卡马克优化中的有效性。

Conclusion: TokaGrad为燃烧等离子体场景和反应堆概念的自动化、可微优化提供了可行路径，标志着聚变模拟从黑箱优化向基于内部梯度的直接优化转变。

Abstract: As fusion energy moves from theoretical feasibility toward commercialization, design of new reactor concepts, autonomous tokamak control, and high-performance scenario optimization are becoming increasingly important. Traditionally, such optimization tasks have relied on costly trial-and-error or brute-force parameter searches, based on black-box experiments or simulations. Recently, advances in differentiable programming are changing the paradigm of numerical simulation. Unlike conventional simulations, which are typically executed as locally connected step-by-step procedures, differentiable simulation represents the entire simulation pipeline as a connected computational graph. In such a framework, machine parameters, actuator waveforms, and plasma responses are linked through differentiable operations, allowing Jacobians to propagate across the full simulation. This enables direct gradient-based control and optimization using the internal sensitivities of the simulator, rather than treating the simulator as a black box. Here, we present TokaGrad, an end-to-end differentiable tokamak transport simulator for full-scenario modeling, including ramp-up, L-mode operation, and H-mode access. TokaGrad self-consistently integrates differentiable models for plasma equilibrium, transport, heating, L-H transition, and pedestal formation. To our knowledge, this is the first differentiable tokamak simulator capable of self-consistently modeling dynamic full-discharge scenarios where actuators and plasma evolve together with equilibrium, pedestal, and confinement-regime transitions. We demonstrate that, when coupled to gradient-based optimizers, TokaGrad enables reactor-design optimization, actuator control, and full-scenario waveform optimization. This framework provides a pathway toward automated, differentiable optimization of burning-plasma scenarios and reactor concepts.

</details>


### [131] [Entanglement entropy of ground states of the Landau Hamiltonian on the half-plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09467)
*Paul Pfeiffer,Wolfgang Spitzer*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究边界条件对基态纠缠熵的影响，发现尽管朗道哈密顿量在半平面上的谱由纯点谱变为绝对连续谱，但其基态仍然满足严格的面积律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究边界条件如何改变哈密顿量的谱性质，以及这种改变对基态纠缠熵标度行为的影响，特别关注绝对连续谱是否必然导致对数增强的面积律。

Method: 对比分析全平面（纯点谱）与半平面（绝对连续谱）上朗道哈密顿量的基态纠缠熵，并与拉普拉斯算子的行为进行对比。

Result: 边界条件可以根本性地改变谱性质（从纯点谱变为绝对连续谱），但基态纠缠熵的领先阶行为（严格面积律）保持不变。

Conclusion: 绝对连续谱并不足以保证对数增强的面积律，需要更精细的条件来解释拉普拉斯算子与朗道哈密顿量之间的行为差异。

Abstract: We study the entanglement entropy (EE) of ground states of a Hamiltonian defined on a domain with a boundary. Surprisingly, boundary conditions can change the spectrum and the nature of the spectrum drastically but not the leading behaviour of the EE. As is well-known, the Landau Hamiltonian on the full plane has pure point spectrum (the infinitely degenerate Landau levels) and ground states display a so-called strict area law. On the other hand, the Landau Hamiltonian on the half-plane has purely absolutely continuous spectrum and yet we prove a strict area law for its ground states. We raise the question of what extra or finer conditions on the absolutely continuous spectrum are necessary to guarantee a logarithmically enhanced area-law as we have for the Laplace operator.

</details>


### [132] [Extended group analysis and conservation laws of a class of variable coefficient generalized Kawahara equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09482)
*Olena Vaneeva,Alexander Zhalij,Olena Magda,Oksana Brahinets*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述并扩展了含时系数广义Kawahara方程组的群分析结果，包括Lie对称性、变换性质、Lie约化、精确解及守恒律分类，并通过收缩联系各对称扩展情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对含时系数广义Kawahara方程进行完整的群分析，补充现有文献在Lie对称性分类、变换性质描述及守恒律分类方面的不足，并建立对称扩展情形之间的收缩联系。

Method: 利用Lie群分析方法，结合等价变换和容许变换，在不简化系数的情况下完成完整的Lie对称性分类；通过Lie约化构造精确解；利用守恒律理论对低阶局部守恒律进行分类；引入收缩分析联系不同对称扩展情形。

Result: 获得了完整的Lie对称性分类；构造了若干精确解；守恒律分类表明所有方程具有质量和L²-范数守恒律，而能量型守恒律仅存在于与对称分类一致的特定系数分支中；收缩分析将对称扩展情形、约化和守恒律联系起来。

Conclusion: 完成了含时系数广义Kawahara方程组的系统群分析，揭示了对称性、守恒律与系数分支之间的内在联系，收缩分析为不同情形间的转换提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We review and extend the results on the group analysis of a class of generalized Kawahara equations with time-dependent coefficients. First, we provide an overview of the existing literature on Lie symmetries and Lie-invariant solutions of such equations. We then present a complete description of the transformation properties of these equations, including admissible, equivalence, and Lie symmetry transformations. For practical applications, the results are further extended by presenting a complete Lie symmetry classification without simplifying the coefficients by equivalence transformations. The Lie reductions are then thoroughly performed and some Lie exact solutions are constructed. The local conservation laws of low order are classified: every equation from the class admits the mass and the $L^2$-norm conservation laws, whereas energy-type conservation laws exist only for distinguished coefficient branches that agree with the cases singled out by the symmetry classification. The classification results are enhanced by a study of contractions, which link cases of Lie symmetry extensions together with the associated reductions and conservation laws.

</details>


### [133] [The Fock Space Dynamics of Causal Fermion Systems: Non-Abelian Gauge Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08488)
*Claudio Dappiaggi,Felix Finster,Niky Kamran,Moritz Reintjes*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了因果作用量原理在闵氏时空下的一个极限情形，该情形产生了包含非阿贝尔规范场和狄拉克场的微扰量子场论的线性Fock空间动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在从因果费米子系的基础理论出发，通过具体极限情形的计算，建立因果作用量原理与标准微扰量子场论之间的联系，为理解量子场论的底层几何与代数结构提供新的视角。

Method: 选取闵可夫斯基时空作为背景，考虑因果作用量原理的某个极限情形，通过严格分析推导出该极限下的动力学方程，并展示其等价于包含非阿贝尔规范场和狄拉克场的微扰QFT的Fock空间表述。

Result: 成功在该极限情形下，从因果费米子系的因果作用量原理导出了线性Fock空间动力学，该动力学涵盖了非阿贝尔规范场（如杨-米尔斯场）和狄拉克费米子的微扰处理。

Conclusion: 该工作表明因果费米子系理论在特定极限下能够还原出标准微扰量子场论的核心动力学结构，为因果费米子系作为量子场论的基础理论提供了支持。

Abstract: A limiting case is worked out in which the causal action principle for causal fermion systems describing Minkowski space gives rise to the linear Fock space dynamics of perturbative quantum field theory including non-abelian gauge fields and Dirac fields.

</details>
